--- entity_google_reviews [row 1 of 5] ---
review_id: 4086
entity_slug: fish-river-grill
google_review_name: places/ChIJ3WT8K9AEmogRnEAtvUne_Ms/reviews/Ci9DQUlRQUNvZENodHljRjlvT210Q2QyOVdRMmhvZFVSWlZsZDFhbVpSYm5SclRYYxAB
rating: 5
review_text: Eating here was a nice experience and I was pleasantly surprised at how fresh and flavorful the food tasted! There was no wait when we got there around 5 PM or so, but the crowd started trickling in soon afterwards. It took no time for the waitress to take our order, get our drinks and appetizers, and our entrees within a reasonable amount of time. All of the entrees we had served large portions, so it's definitely a good place if you have a large appetite!

They start you with the swamp soup regardless of the entree...and I have to say, it was unexpectedly good especially considering the dead simple ingredients. We followed up with the Cheese Curds and the Gator bites. Both were delicious, flavorful, juicy, and fresh!

I had the relatively safe option for the entree: a River Cheeseburger sans pickles or mustard (personal preference) with a side of fries. The burger was larger than I was expecting and I couldn't finish it in one sitting, but it was pretty good. I bummed a piece of the fried catfish and like the Gator Bites, the breading was well seasoned and flavorful.

I definitely would recommend this place if you're looking for a hole-in-the-whole local place to go out and have a bit with family and friends. It also has a bar too if hanging out and watching sports is your thing too.
language_code: en
author_name: David Allen

--- entity_reviews [row 1 of 5] ---
id: 32d6f159-04c9-48bf-9374-f287ed6f6ac5
entity_slug: fish-river-grill
reviewer_name: David Allen
rating: 5
body: Eating here was a nice experience and I was pleasantly surprised at how fresh and flavorful the food tasted! There was no wait when we got there around 5 PM or so, but the crowd started trickling in soon afterwards. It took no time for the waitress to take our order, get our drinks and appetizers, and our entrees within a reasonable amount of time. All of the entrees we had served large portions, so it's definitely a good place if you have a large appetite!

They start you with the swamp soup regardless of the entree...and I have to say, it was unexpectedly good especially considering the dead simple ingredients. We followed up with the Cheese Curds and the Gator bites. Both were delicious, flavorful, juicy, and fresh!

I had the relatively safe option for the entree: a River Cheeseburger sans pickles or mustard (personal preference) with a side of fries. The burger was larger than I was expecting and I couldn't finish it in one sitting, but it was pretty good. I bummed a piece of the fried catfish and like the Gator Bites, the breading was well seasoned and flavorful.

I definitely would recommend this place if you're looking for a hole-in-the-whole local place to go out and have a bit with family and friends. It also has a bar too if hanging out and watching sports is your thing too.
verified_purchase: false
helpful_count: 0
approved: true
created_at: 2025-12-01T23:41:55.008646+00:00
